# Re:Zero — master longitudinal ledger

## 0. Authority, history, and use

This is the canonical cumulative state ledger for the Re:Zero Japanese main-light-novel analysis. It is mutable current authority through the latest verified volume transaction. Frozen volume readings remain the authority for what was supportable at each historical prospective horizon and for their detailed source-facing arguments.

This ledger was created after V02 during the project-initiation architecture repair. The V01/V02 state below is a controlled backfill from the already frozen canonical readings; the ledger did not govern those readings prospectively and neither freeze was modified. Exact source evidence was consulted only where necessary to validate the source identities already recorded by those artifacts.

For every new volume, update this file only after the source-facing reading has identified the material transitions. Preserve revision ancestry and route to the relevant VNN section or exact EPUB locus. Do not duplicate an independently promoted ledger here beyond a routing/current-summary row.

### Preserved predecessor claims without a V03 replacement

Through V03, V01 C011/C012/C014/C015/C017/C018/C020/C021 and V02 C009/C013/C022/C023/C028 remained effective under the recorded scope. At V04, Felt/Reinhard and selection routes advance under C021–C027/C031; V01's “Satella” naming motive, Elsa employer and Felt blessing explanation remain unresolved. V02-E3 terminal attribution remains historical inference, not reassigned by the new court evidence. V04 §12 records the older-claim review. No earlier character acquires later knowledge retroactively.

| ワタシ | Remembered creator to Beatrice | The mother's self-reference is retained with the remembered commissioning context, not flattened into every later Echidna voice. | p-001#B0003–0017; p-018#B0003–0038 |
| 私 → 僕 | Roswaal's private confession to Beatrice | The shift accompanies disclosure of original-disciple continuity; public current role and private old familiarity are not interchangeable. | p-022#B0104–0135 |
| 君が憎い | Echidna to Emilia | Actual hatred is delivered near tears. The pronouncement is evidence of hostility, not its whole cause or a diagnosis. | p-004#B0254–0292 |
| 不可能 / possible-future framing | Second versus third trial | Impossible family continuation and contingent calamity images require distinct representational statuses; not every depicted future is a forecast certified true. | p-008#B0003–0124/B0166–0168 |
| 大切な子 | Emilia's valuation of Subaru before the guide | A dear/important person formulation does not become an own reciprocal romantic love confession. | p-008#B0125–0221 |
| 母 / foster father and brother roles | Emilia's impossible family present | Lived care and chosen family roles do not identify biological maternity, paternity or a legal marriage. | p-004#B0003–0028/B0170–0172/B0205–0242 |
| ツノアリ | Meili's rare responsive beasts | Having a horn is compatible with the separately described release from its binding; do not translate release as physical removal without evidence. | p-001#B0319–0339; p-007#B0107–0131; p-013#B0059–0103 |
| ママ | Elsa/Meili handler context | The punitive work-role address remains unidentified. The spelling and dependence do not supply biological motherhood or Roswaal's identity. | p-001#B0319–0339; p-007#B0107–0131; p-013#B0059–0103 |
| 吸血鬼 | Garfiel's book-derived category/Elsa acknowledgment | A locally applied category is not a complete universal resurrection taxonomy or exact fixed life count. | p-013#B0092–0101/B0153–0203 |
| 千里眼 | Ram's Roswaal-attuned sight | This supports the concealed strike, not thought reading, all-private receipt or full RBD knowledge. | p-017#B0032–0060/B0136–0175 |
| 鬼化 | Ram's very brief incomplete activation | Duration and bodily tearing are essential; translation cannot silently supply restored horn or full prime state. | p-017#B0032–0060/B0136–0175 |
| 星獣化 | Puck's decoy | The enlarged cute form in this fight is a deception, not an executed full catastrophic transformation or unlimited power. | p-017#B0032–0040/B0232–0280; p-024#B0003–0017 |
| こきゅーとす | Emilia's Subaru-taught technique name | The naming receipt is distinct from self-invented expert taxonomy and from universal control over all cold magic. | p-020#B0078–0089; p-021#B0130–0157/B0225–0229 |
| アル・シャマク | Beatrice in joint battle | The actual outcome is separate-dimension displacement; a convenient gloss such as extermination would erase the Rabbit focal aftermath. | p-021#B0083–0109/B0170–0215 |
| 契約 / 盟約 / 誓約 | Beatrice's explanation after the wager | Two-party, bloodline and individual commitments are distinguished; a translated generic contract cannot erase bearer and enforcement differences. | p-024#B0106–0169 |
| 誓約の呪印 | Visible Roswaal mark and claimed penalty | Visibility corroborates a mark, not a tested inability to act. Later willingness to incur punishment is crucial counterevidence. | p-024#B0106–0169 |
| ケジメ / interest metaphor | Household blows and Otto's settlement language | Ritual reckoning is not complete repayment or a public legal judgment; the material principal remains unsettled. | p-024#B0080–0105/B0169–0239 |
| ごめんなさい | Emilia's demanded apology | An actual apologetic act is required beyond oath/penalty. This Roswaal apology is not Subaru's separate missing overnight apology. | p-024#B0080–0105/B0169–0239 |
| 愚者 / 賢人 | Roswaal's private perfect-save pressure | His contrast frames Subaru's dilemma and continuing coercion; the analyst need not endorse its exhaustive two-option morality. | p-027#B0077–0107 |
| 今度は破らないで | Emilia's new finger promise | Not this time keeps the earlier breach active while enabling future trust; it does not retrospectively furnish apology or fulfilled origin disclosure. | p-027#B0154–0208/B0223–0228 |
| オメガ | Departing Witch's chosen alias | The direct alias is firmer than the contextual full identity mapping; knowing the name does not belong to the absent cast. | p-028#B0002–0037 |
| 第四章完結 | Afterword and backmatter | This explicitly certifies a source arc boundary. It is publication paratext, not a narrative act, model fidelity verdict or license to open an advertised witness. | p-029#B0004/B0014/B0016; p-033#B0001–0008; p-colophon#B0004–0008 |
